blah blah blah none of that is right in my opinion none of that is right because you have to look at a covered call collectively you have to look at a cover call as a package deal you can't look at just the long stock you can't look at just the short call they are forever linked like they are husband and wife they are one flesh now and so it is absolutely unequivocally a package deal and so once you get into a covered call like whether you put it on as a cover call from the beginning or maybe you leg into it after the fact whatever it doesn't matter once you have that on when you're trying to make decisions and analyze where you're at you have to take both sides into account because I look at a day like today with Starbucks up I'm like this is a good day for me it doesn't matter that I'm down on the short call like that's the trade that I made like that's the exchange that I was okay with like in exchange for bringing some premium in in exchange for giving us you know giving myself a higher probability on the trade like that is the thing that I am doing with this overall position and so in my opinion and this actually applies to any option strategy when you have multiple legs on different sides of the market you can really start you know spinning yourself in circles and you know find yourself in some real mindbenders if you start just looking at the one side and not the other side i mean take a take a short strangle for example if I'm only looking at one side I'm going to see one side doing amazing and the other side doing not so amazing and so what is the solution to like take off the side that's doing amazing and leave on the side that's not like no not at all because then you're changing the complete spirit and the complexity of the strategy into something that you maybe never wanted to begin with and I've seen this happen over and over and over again with cover calls and so do yourself a favor once you have the cover call in place the stock and the call they go together they are a package deal and so I would look at them that way and that's exactly how I'm looking at this right now and so when you look at the short call and it's up uh meaning down for you
